 <div style="font-size:10pt;width:8.5in;margin:0 auto">
<table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" border="0" style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-weight:bold;border-collapse:collapse;text-align:left;width:100%;font-size:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-top:0pt;border:0;">
<tr style="page-break-inside:avoid;">
<td style="width:10%;vertical-align:top;white-space:nowrap;">Item&#160;8.01 </td>
<td style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-weight:bold;text-align:left;font-size:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-top:0pt;">Other Events.</td> </tr> </table> <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;text-align:left;font-size:10pt;margin-bottom:0px;margin-top:6pt">On September&#160;3, 2019, The Walt Disney Company (&#8220;<span style="text-decoration:underline">Disney</span>&#8221;) issued a press release announcing (a)&#160;its intention to offer one or more series of its floating rate senior unsecured notes and fixed rate senior unsecured notes (collectively, the &#8220;<span style="text-decoration:underline">New Notes</span>&#8221;) in a registered public offering (the &#8220;<span style="text-decoration:underline">Offering</span>&#8221;) pursuant to Disney&#8217;s shelf registration statement on Form <span style="white-space:nowrap">S-3,</span> filed with the Securities and Exchange Commission on September&#160;3, 2019, and (b)&#160;the commencement of cash tender offers (the &#8220;<span style="text-decoration:underline">Tender Offers</span>&#8221;) by Disney and its indirect subsidiary, 21st Century Fox America, Inc. (&#8220;<span style="text-decoration:underline">21CFA</span>&#8221;), to purchase certain outstanding notes of Disney, subject to a maximum aggregate purchase price (including principal and premium, but excluding accrued interest) of $1,750,000,000 (as may be modified by Disney), and any and all outstanding debt securities of 21CFA. </p> <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;text-align:left;font-size:10pt;margin-bottom:0px;margin-top:12pt">The Tender Offers are subject to the conditions described in the offer to purchase and related letter of transmittal dated September&#160;3, 2019, including the condition that Disney has received net proceeds of at least $2,000,000,000 from the Offering of New Notes. The Tender Offers will expire at 11:59 p.m., New York City time, on September 30, 2019, unless extended or earlier terminated. </p> <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;text-align:left;font-size:10pt;margin-bottom:0px;margin-top:12pt">A copy of the press release is attached hereto as Exhibit 99.1 and is incorporated herein by reference. </p> <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;text-align:left;font-size:10pt;margin-bottom:0px;margin-top:12pt">This Current Report on Form <span style="white-space:nowrap">8-K</span> is not an offer to purchase or sell or a solicitation of an offer to purchase or sell, with respect to any securities. </p> <p style="margin-bottom:0px;margin-top:18pt"></p>

<Center><DIV STYLE="width:8.5in" align="left">
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="right"><B>Exhibit 99.1 </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">FOR IMMEDIATE RELEASE </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B>THE WALT DISNEY
COMPANY ANNOUNCES COMMENCEMENT OF </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B>SENIOR UNSECURED NOTES OFFERING BY </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B>THE WALT DISNEY COMPANY </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B>AND CONCURRENT TENDER OFFERS BY </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B>THE WALT DISNEY COMPANY AND 21ST CENTURY FOX AMERICA, INC. </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">BURBANK, Calif., September&nbsp;3, 2019 &#150; The Walt Disney Company (&#147;<U>Disney</U>&#148;) (NYSE: DIS) announced today the commencement of an offering
(the &#147;<U>Offering</U>&#148;) of one or more series of its floating rate senior unsecured notes and fixed rate senior unsecured notes (collectively, the &#147;<U>New Notes</U>&#148;) pursuant to an effective shelf registration statement filed
with the Securities and Exchange Commission (the &#147;<U>SEC</U>&#148;). The New Notes would be guaranteed by TWDC Enterprises 18 Corp., a subsidiary of Disney. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Disney intends to use the net proceeds from the Offering (i)&nbsp;to pay the cash consideration in the Tender Offers (as defined below), subject, in the case
of the tender offer for the Disney Notes (as defined below), to the Maximum Disney Tender Cap (as defined below), any accrued and unpaid interest with respect to the Tender Offers and related fees and expenses, (ii)&nbsp;prepay in full the aggregate
principal amount outstanding under the <FONT STYLE="white-space:nowrap">364-Day</FONT> Credit Agreement, dated as of March&nbsp;15, 2019, among Disney, as the borrower, the lenders party thereto, Citibank, N.A., as a
<FONT STYLE="white-space:nowrap">co-administrative</FONT> agent, and JPMorgan Chase Bank, N.A., as a <FONT STYLE="white-space:nowrap">co-administrative</FONT> agent and as the designated agent, and (iii)&nbsp;for general corporate purposes. The
Offering is not conditioned on the completion of the Tender Offers. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><I>The Tender Offers </I></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Concurrently with the commencement of the Offering, Disney and its indirect subsidiary, 21st Century Fox America, Inc. (&#147;<U>21CFA</U>&#148;), have
commenced cash tender offers (each, a &#147;<U>Tender Offer</U>&#148;) to purchase outstanding notes of Disney listed in the table below (the &#147;<U>Disney Notes</U>&#148;), subject to the Maximum Disney Tender Cap and in the order of priority
shown in the table below, and any and all outstanding debt securities of 21CFA (the &#147;<U>21CFA Notes</U>&#148; and together with the Disney Notes, the &#147;<U>Tender Notes</U>&#148; and each a &#147;<U>Series</U>&#148; of Tender Notes). </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B><U>The Disney Notes </U></B></P> <P STYLE="font-size:12pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>

<HR SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">


<Center><DIV STYLE="width:8.5in" align="left">
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The terms and conditions of the Tender Offers are described in a separate Offer to Purchase dated
September&nbsp;3,&nbsp;2019 (as it may be amended or supplemented, the &#147;<U>Offer to Purchase</U>&#148;) and related Letter of Transmittal (as it may be amended or supplemented, the &#147;<U>Letter of Transmittal</U>&#148;). The Tender Offers
are subject to the satisfaction of certain conditions as set forth in the Offer to Purchase, including the receipt by Disney prior to the Expiration Date (or Early Settlement Date, if the purchasers elect to have an early settlement) of net proceeds
of at least $2,000,000,000 from the Offering. Subject to applicable law, the purchasers may waive any and all of these conditions or extend, terminate or withdraw the Tender Offer with respect to one or more Series of Tender Notes and/or increase or
decrease or eliminate the Maximum Disney Tender Cap. The Tender Offers are not conditioned upon any minimum amount of Tender Notes being tendered or the consummation of any Tender Offer in respect of any Series of Tender Notes. Capitalized terms
used and not defined in this press release have the meanings given to them in the Offer to Purchase. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The amounts of each Series of Disney Notes that are
accepted for purchase in the Tender Offer will be determined in accordance with the priorities identified in the column &#147;Acceptance Priority Level&#148; in the table above. Disney&#146;s obligation to accept for purchase, based on the
Acceptance Priority Levels, and to pay for, Disney Notes that are validly tendered and not validly withdrawn is limited to as many of the Disney Notes as Disney can purchase up to an aggregate purchase price (including principal and premium, but
excluding accrued interest) of $1,750,000,000 (as such amount may be increased, decreased or eliminated pursuant to the terms of the Offer to Purchase, the &#147;<U>Maximum Disney Tender Cap</U>&#148;). </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Any and all 21CFA Notes validly tendered and not validly withdrawn at or prior to the Early Tender Deadline, or validly tendered and not validly withdrawn
after the Early Tender Deadline but at or prior to the Expiration Date, as applicable, will be accepted for purchase on the applicable Settlement Date, regardless of (a)&nbsp;the aggregate purchase price of such 21CFA Notes or (b)&nbsp;the
application of the Acceptance Priority Levels or the Maximum Disney Tender Cap. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The Tender Offers will expire at 11:59 p.m., New York City time, on
September 30, 2019 (as the same may be extended with respect to one or more Series of Tender Notes, the &#147;<U>Expiration Date</U>&#148;). In order to receive the applicable Total Consideration, Holders of Tender Notes must validly tender and not
validly withdraw their Tender Notes before the Early Tender Deadline, which is 5:00 p.m., New York City time, on September&nbsp;16, 2019 (as the same may be extended with respect to one or more Series of Tender Notes, the &#147;<U>Early Tender
Deadline</U>&#148;). Holders of Tender Notes who validly tender their Tender Notes after the Early Tender Deadline and before the Expiration Date and whose Tender Notes are accepted for purchase will receive only the applicable Purchase Price, as
fully described in the Offer to Purchase. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The applicable Total Consideration for each $1,000 in principal amount of Tender Notes tendered and not
withdrawn before the Early Tender Deadline and accepted for purchase pursuant to the Tender Offers will be determined in the manner described in the Offer to Purchase. The consideration will be determined by reference to a fixed spread specified for
each Series of Tender Notes over the yield based on the bid price of the applicable Reference Security specified in the table above, as fully described in the Offer to Purchase. The consideration will be calculated by the Dealer Managers (as defined
below) at 10:00 A.M., New York City time, on September&nbsp;17, 2019. The applicable Early Tender Premium for each Series of Tender Notes is set forth in the table above. The Purchase Price for the Tender Notes accepted for purchase pursuant to the
Tender Offers will be calculated by taking the Total Consideration for the applicable Series of Tender Notes and subtracting from it the Early Tender Premium for such Series. In addition to the applicable Total Consideration or applicable Purchase
Price, as the case may be, accrued and unpaid interest from the last interest payment date up to, but not including, the applicable Settlement Date will be paid in cash on all validly tendered Tender Notes accepted for purchase in the Tender Offers.
</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The purchasers reserve the right, but are under no obligation, at any point following the Early Tender Deadline and before the Expiration Date, to accept
Tender Notes that have been validly tendered and not validly withdrawn for purchase on a date determined at their option (such date, if any, the &#147;<U>Early Settlement Date</U>&#148;). The Total Consideration plus accrued interest for Tender
Notes that are validly tendered and not validly withdrawn on or before the Early Tender Deadline and accepted for purchase will be paid by the purchasers in <FONT STYLE="white-space:nowrap">same-day</FONT> funds on such Early Settlement Date, if
any, which is expected to occur promptly following the Early Tender Deadline, on September&nbsp;18,&nbsp;2019. The Purchase Price plus accrued interest for Tender Notes that are validly tendered after the
